The mend of sitting 72 (tag 6692; held: the Coin flip board said "Nobody has won a flip against Ace yet." before the answer came, because the flip's store starts as an empty board): every board hands the worker's answer, or none, to one rule (ranksPage.js rankAnswer), so "not answered yet" and "answered, and empty" are told apart on every board (the ranked ones, a failed fetch being no answer; the spin, the flip, the fish); a board or group the list lacks waits for the worker's list before it says "nobody" (rankListWaiting).
- **A tap on another player's row opens their Character page**, named as theirs ("Bee's Bro"), read-only, fed ONLY by the board's row (ranksPage.js passportFromRow): their name, face, the board's own number, Sword, Bow and Staff levels, the combat level (the best weapon's, the worker's `lv`), AP, kills, the clan tag; the title and quests "Not shared" (never one of your values, never "No title equipped" for a title the game was not told). Nothing on it opens anything: the weapons, Titles and Journey read only your state (step 9 brings another player's full page). Your own row opens your one Bro page.
- **Back returns to the same board at the same place**: the board is the frame's entry's `sel`, and the frame keeps that visit's scroll. The Menu resumes the board last open. The weigh-in card's Records lands on Biggest fish at the kind weighed, under Feats.
- **Every header whole** (v2.3.6846; the owner, 2026-11-28, on a phone: *"Leaderboards menu cuts off headers"*): the board's heads (Rank, Player, the unit) sat low in their 18 px strip at every size, their foot cut ("Plaver"), because the face column's empty head kept a row's 30 px face box and Rank a row's 15 px number; the heads now keep their own 11 px word (12 under `?v17look`), Rank like Player. A door's word too wide for its tile ("Points placed", "Combat level" at 390 and under, "Woodcutting" at 375 and under) spilled past the tile or lost its end; it now wraps onto a second line (at its space, else where the phone hyphenates it), never smaller, still five across. Kept as it was: Back's long word ending in an ellipsis at 320 wide (the kit's rule above). `mp-ranksheads` measures every header at 390 x 844, 375 x 667, 320 x 568, 430 x 932 and 844 x 390.

**What the real size could not hold:** upright at 390 x 844 the board holds the heads and about three rows; sideways (about 63 px) one row and no heads, the choice beside the list. Another player's page upright holds the identity line and the facts in two columns; sideways three.

**Flagged for the owner:** another player's FULL page from the worker (gear, stats, placed points, a title) is step 9's; a board for the combined life-skill level and an Equipment board wait for the worker to keep the number.

**Tried before:** the Ranks page in four tabs with a picker, the bars and laurels and an always-pinned row (v2.3.5503), and the list-and-podium page before it: both kept under `?ui2=0` (`?ui2=0&rankstyle=0` the older).

Sources: src/game/ranksPage.js (ranksGroups, ranksBoardHeads, youPinned, passportFromRow, ranksOpens, rankSel, ranksEntry), src/ui/mobile/dash/RanksLanding.jsx, RankStandings.jsx, rankViews.jsx, ranksUi2.css, rankWords.js, LeaderboardPanel.jsx (the choice), src/ui/mobile/sheet/CharacterPage.jsx (OtherCharacter), BottomDashboard.jsx (PANELS ranksGroup, ranksPoints, ranksBoard; titleOf), src/data/say/leaderboard.js; tools/guards/ranks-ui2.test.mjs, ranks-mount.test.mjs; tools/qa/mp/mp-ui2ranks.mjs.

## Feats (v2.3.6695, card UI2-feats, the build's step 6)
Screens: dash-bro-achievements, ui2-feats-tiers, ui2-feats-gallery, ui2-feats-medal, ui2-feats-ap, ui2-feats-quests

**What it is for:** your achievement points large, and where every point came from as far as the game truly knows; your medals by the game's own four tiers, earned ones bright and the rest muted but readable; and one door each to your titles, the quests you finished and your lifetime records. The owner, 2026-11-25: *"Feats is the approved replacement for Accomplishments."* The master specification 4.5: *"AP (Achievement Points) is the dominant headline of Feats, with a drill-down into its actual sources."*; *"Do not assume all these categories award AP; use the actual achievement model. Maintain the original consolidation and reuse existing records/quest data."*; *"Earned medals have their full appearance; unearned medals are muted but inspectable. Use non-color state cues."*; *"Do not silently alter achievement requirements or AP values."*; *"Standalone titles appear as concise rows with their own requirements; never fabricate a progression relation."*; *"Exact headline records and units depend on actual tracked data. Do not invent retroactive tracking."* Their standing rule: *"Yeah my game content over the mockup stuff"*. And after playing the new dashboard, 2026-11-26 (docs/design/UI_FEEDBACK_2026-11-26.md part 2): *"Titles could live within Feats."*

**Decisions, and why:**
- **The page id stays `achievements`**, so every road in lands on Feats: Bro's Feats tile, the strip's AP, More's old road, the world's title chip. Under the frame it is one of Bro's own pages (header "Feats", no head row of its own); under `?ui2=0` today's Achievements page, untouched.
- **AP is the stat** (the kit's sunk surface, a large gold number): the worker's total, never the medals added up, and the kit's loading placeholder until the worker's record has come (never a 0). It is a door to its sources only when the game can say them.
- **AP's sources, by the stat tree's own page** (one layer at a time, as DPS's): the total = From achievements (by tier, then each earned medal, a leaf that opens its page) + **From quests, guild rungs and the arena**, ONE row: the worker keeps one total, so the phone can split it in two and no further, and that row never names one of the three alone. When the parts do not add up (a row lit by the owner's test lever pays nothing), there is **no tree**: the line "Where these came from is not known", never a split that is false.
- **Four equal raised doors** under AP, two by two upright, one row sideways with AP first: Achievements ("n of 33"), Titles (the title you wear, "Wearing: Blooded"), Quest History, Lifetime Records. **No Discoveries door**: the worker keeps none (the four old lists are the retired map's, in the phone's storage).
- **The medals are the game's four tiers**, each its own (the arena is NOT filed under Rare, as the old page's chip did; never Gold, Silver, Bronze): Common, Rare, Arena, Super-rare, each with "n of N" and its points earned of its points possible by each row's own points (the ten fish rows pay their own 5 and 10). No tier picture exists: a tier wears its latest earned badge, or, with none, its first badge muted with a lock, ringed in its colour (grey, blue, the arena's orange, Super-rare's prismatic ring).
- **A tier's gallery**: its badges in the table's order, two rows upright, one sideways, each 44 px or more; earned ones full, the rest the kit's muted tile with a lock mark (a cue that is not colour) and "Not earned" for a screen reader. Every badge opens its page.
- **A medal's page**: the badge and its name beside the kit's FACTS in one flat container: its tier, its AP, the day it was earned or "Not earned yet", its requirement word for word, how far along you are where the game has a hint (the three combat rows, the two codex rows, the dungeons) and nothing where it has none (never "0 of 1"), and, earned and yours, "Title: <name>", a door to Titles.
- **Titles is Character's one Titles page** (Equip and Remove, the pending mark), never a second list, and Feats' Titles door is now its ONLY home for yourself: the Bro page's strip lost its title entry (the owner, 2026-11-26). Nothing is lost: the worn title is on the Titles door's line, at the head of the Titles page and on its medal's page. The page gains two things: the worn title's requirement under it ("how a title was earned"), and the game's one family, **Blooded, Veteran, Centurion** (a weapon to level 10, 50 and 100: one requirement, three levels), as one joined row of three, got, next and locked each by a word and a mark; every other title stays a row of its own (no relation is made up).
- **Quest History** is the Quests pages' own Completed list, called as it is (`DonePane`, `questJournalList`): the AP your hand-ins paid and each quest with its +5; a row opens that quest's page by the Quests pages' own road.
- **Lifetime Records opens the one Journey page** (two doors, one page: Bro's Journey tile is the other; flagged below). Under the frame its record cards are only the counters the game truly keeps: kills (the worker's when it sends them), deaths, lifetime gold, lifetime XP.
- **Another player's Feats** (nothing opens one yet: step 9): the AP and title the worker stamps on their entry, and "Not shared" on medals, quests and records (their phone's); no Equip.
- **The script**: Feats rides the same lazily loaded script as today's Achievements page, so nothing new loads before you open it and no memory line rises; a stale tab says "Couldn't open this" as before (mp-lazyfail).

**What the real size could not hold:** sideways the AP's label is "AP" ("Achievement points" was cut at 667 x 375) and the four doors' words take two lines ("Lifetime Records"); a tier with more medals than six in AP's tree shows five and a door, "n more", to the tier's gallery; the Titles page's list scrolls as it always has when the titles are many (33 cannot fit 172 px).

**Flagged for the owner:** Duels Won and Deepest Zone are drawn on today's Journey page and never counted (always 0 and "shallow"): count them, or take them off? Not drawn under the frame until then. AP beyond two sources needs the worker to keep its sources apart (a server change); a medal's progress beyond the game's few hints; Discoveries (a decision to track them); another player's records. Missing art: a picture for each tier. The three combat medals' words say "Melee, Bow or Magic" where the new screens say Sword, Bow, Staff: content, left as it is. Lifetime Records and Bro's Journey tile open the same page (two doors): keep both?

**Tried before:** the Achievements page (v2.3.3527): 33 rows with filter chips (the arena's row under Rare), the AP and count on the drilled header row, the title chosen inside a row; kept under `?ui2=0`.

Sources: src/game/feats.js, src/data/say/feats.js, src/ui/mobile/sheet/FeatsPage.jsx, MedalTiers.jsx, MedalPage.jsx, QuestHistory.jsx, TitleFamily.jsx, featsGo.js, feats.css, StatTree.jsx (ApTree), CharacterPage.jsx (TitlesPage), src/ui/mobile/dash/JourneyPanel.jsx, AchievementsPanel.jsx, src/game/metricTree.js, src/game/broHub.js, src/game/dashDests.js; tools/guards/feats.test.mjs, feats-mount.test.mjs; tools/qa/mp/mp-ui2feats.mjs.
## Life Skills under the new dashboard (v2.3.6694, card UI2-life, the build's step 6)
Screens: ui2-life-landing, ui2-life-skill, ui2-life-unlocks, dash-bro-skill-detail

**What it is for:** one number for how far you have come across all ten crafts, ten doors that each show how near the next level is, and a skill's own page that tells you exactly what each future level opens, so you can plan. The owner's master specification, 2026-11-25, 4.4: *"Sum of life skill levels as headline statistic, encouraging growth across skills. This is a separate measure from combat level"*; *"Each skill destination includes a compact XP progress bar so players can see proximity to the next level without opening every skill."* 3.3: *"Reveal all skill unlocks in the complete roadmap, including future rewards."* And after playing the new dashboard, 2026-11-26 (docs/design/UI_FEEDBACK_2026-11-26.md part 4): *"Life Skills. Enlarge the icons and cards to occupy the unused vertical space. Put level indicators inside the cards instead of having badges protrude from them. Match the rest of the game's typography."*

**Decisions, and why:**
- **The page id stays `skills`**: Bro's Life Skills door, L on a computer, More's road and every test that opens `skills` land on the new landing; under `?ui2=0` today's page (the home screen of v2.3.3522) is drawn instead.
- **"Total level" is the stat** (the kit's three weights, the owner's "Important statistics: Recessed surfaces with large numbers"): sunk, its number large and gold, opening nothing. It is the sum of the ten numbers on the tiles, each read as its tile and the worker read it (a skill is never 0), over the ten only (the record also holds pets, gems and more). Bro's old card summed a missing skill as 0; it reads 1 now, as its tile does.
- **The ten are raised doors that fill the page** (the owner's "occupy the unused vertical space"): upright five across and two down under the total, sideways one row of eleven with the total the first cell; the gather row over the craft row, never resorted. Each picture takes every pixel the words leave. On the test box at 390 x 844 the page is 191 px tall: the total's row about 30, two gaps of 4, two rows of 76 px.
- **"Lv N" is inside the face, among the tile's words** (the owner's "level indicators inside the cards instead of having badges protrude"), on one row with the thin XP bar; the brass dot of a level-up not yet looked at sits inside the tile's edge. No badge carries a level. The dot is read before the page marks the level-ups seen, so it shows once.
- **The tile's word is the navigation size** (13 px, the kit's one serif; the owner's "Match the rest of the game's typography"): no size of the page's own.
- **A skill's page** (header its name): its level and XP (now / the worker's need at every level, past 100 too), then ONE flat container of facts -- the next unlock, the latest one, the guild's line with a door to Guilds -- and two raised doors, **All unlocks (n)** and **How it works**. A rung is both requirement and benefit ("Lv 5: Clownfish"); several at one level read "+n".
- **All unlocks is the whole ladder**, every rung, Got / Next / Later by a word and a mark, never colour alone: the one Life Skills page that scrolls, opened with the next rung in view. Farming's rungs are its crops at the level each asks to plant. Trapping and Enchanting unlock nothing by level: the kit's empty state with how the skill is earned, never an invented rung.
- **How it works** is today's detail lines, moved whole into one piece both pages draw (sheet/SkillHow.jsx): each hit and the next level's, full and less XP, the benefit, where it is found, how it is earned.
- **Another player's pages** (nothing opens them until step 9) show levels only: no XP, no dot, no How it works. Their levels are what the worker sent of them (`who.lifeSkills`); when it sent none (the Ranks board's row carries none) the page says "Not shared", whole, never 1 each and never yours. The mend of sitting 72 (tag 6694; held: "the card's dodge 'another player's XP shown' is green"): `life-profile.test` C1 to C4 read each drawing's tree (every reading handed the page's `who`; the How it works door and page only yours; "Not shared"), plants Q1 to Q7 each red.
- **No radar, no label of what kind of player you are, no "highest" badge**: the specification's 4.1 leaves what they measure to the owner.

**What the real size could not hold:** at the navigation size "Woodworking" is 91 px, "Woodcutting" 84, "Gem Cutting" 81, "Enchanting" 75, and a tile is 72 to 83 px wide upright, 73 sideways at 844 x 390 and 57 at 667 x 375; so a name that does not fit breaks onto a second line at its joint (Wood|working, Gem Cutting, Trap|ping), and that tile's picture is smaller than a one-line tile's. Sideways (82 px at 844 x 390) the skill page drops its latest unlock (All unlocks has it).

**Flagged for the owner:** what a label or a radar would measure (specification 4.1); a board for the total (the worker would sum its own ten); past level 100 today's tiles (under `?ui2=0`) ask 1.10 a level where the worker asks 1.08 (this page shows the worker's); "Found at" names ("Frost Ridge", "Starting Meadow", "Stone Hollows") are not checked against the Wheel; Woodcutting's and Mining's rungs past 20 are in the game's tables but grow nowhere on the Wheel yet.

**Tried before:** the home screen of ten app tiles with the level on a chip at the corner and the name under the tile (v2.3.3522, kept under `?ui2=0`); pills (v2.3.1312b).

Sources: src/game/lifeProfile.js, src/game/progression.js, src/ui/mobile/sheet/LifeSkillsPage.jsx, LifeSkillPage.jsx, LifeUnlocks.jsx, SkillHow.jsx, lifePage.css, src/data/say/lifePage.js, src/ui/mobile/sheet/skillsModel.js, src/ui/mobile/dash/SkillsPanel.jsx, tools/guards/life-profile.test.mjs, tools/guards/life-mount.test.mjs, tools/qa/mp/mp-ui2life.mjs

## A computer's open band, 840 wide (v2.3.6842, card UI4 PR 3: the owner's V17 handoff)
Screens: v17wide-bro

**What it is for:** the owner's V17 specification of 2026-11-27 draws a computer's open band as *"840 x 243 centered"* (part 2's Desktop row) and says *"In desktop use extra horizontal space for additional entries, not larger art, text or gameplay view"*. The game's block (the header row, the open page and the resting columns, centred in the band) was 840 until v2.3.4243 and 480 since, when the owner said at a computer: *"Simply make the container areas smaller (like they appear on mobile) they don't need a bunch of extra dark space to make them appear wider"*. V17 is the later word, so 840 goes on with its switch, and both words are the owner's to weigh (card UI4, Q9).

**Decisions, and why:**
- **Only the block's width.** `?v17wide` (on by default, remembered; `=0` the 480) sets `BAND_ASSEMBLY_MAX_W` (`src/game/v17Wide.js`). The band's height (243), the header (52), the closed band, the world above it and the camera never read it (`tools/guards/v17-wide.test.mjs`, and `mp-v17fit` measures the world's canvas and the camera's scale the same under 840 and 480 at 1440 x 900). A phone is unchanged: its band is its own width, under both numbers (a phone on its side is never capped).
- **A step beside the keys list.** The short list of keys stands right of the block (the owner, v2.3.4263: *"add the short list of shortcuts to the extra dashboard area on the right"*) and needs about 260 px a side (the block is centred). So on a computer's band the block is 840 only where the band holds 840 and the list (a band of 1360 or more: 1440 x 900, V17's row; 1920 x 1080), and 480 otherwise (a 1280 x 800 laptop's 1226 px band, 1366 x 768, 1024): never a width between, so only the two shapes the owner has seen exist and the old layouts never meet a third (`deskBlockW`; BroTown stamps it as --band-max-w).
- **Words and pictures keep their phone sizes** (measured: each screen's largest and smallest word the same at 840 as at 480).
- **What it does NOT do yet:** use the room for MORE entries. Measured at 1440 x 900, each of the seven first-slice screens keeps its count a row and widens it (the Menu 4 a row, each 204 px against 114; Bro's six and a weapon page's six 3 a row, 276 px against 156; Gear's bag 3 across, its panel wider and emptier). That is each screen's own grid, card UI4's PR 4 (Bro), PR 5 (Combat) and PR 6 (Gear): this PR does not redraw a screen. Until they land, 840 shows wider tiles, which is close to what the owner turned away on 2026-11-05; `?v17wide=0` is the way back.
**Flagged for the owner:** Q9 (840 or 480, and whether to wait for the screens' PRs); a tablet (an iPad, wider than 480) takes the wider block too, as V17 has no tablet row.

Sources: src/game/v17Wide.js, src/ui/mobile/sheet/sheetGeometry.js (BAND_ASSEMBLY_MAX_W), src/styles/game.css (the band-keys block), tools/guards/v17-wide.test.mjs, tools/qa/mp/mp-v17fit.mjs, docs/shots/v17fit-table.md

## The new menus' shell: the dock, the gear, the closes (v2.3.6860, card MN step 1)
Screens: menus3-dock

**What it is for:** the owner, 2026-11-28: *"design the menu system from scratch. Consolidate as many elements into as few menus as possible while staying coherent"*; their document and wireframe are `docs/design/menus3/` (SPEC.md, WIREFRAME.html), and what they said yes to is `DECISIONS.md` there. Three menus on a bottom dock (Bro: Bag, Power, Skills; World: Quests, Codex, Feats; People: Roster, Chat, Ranks), the Map from the minimap, Settings from a gear, every entry always opening the same place. Step 1 is the shell: the dock, the shortcuts, the close rules and the panel's container; each section is rebuilt by its own later step.

**Decisions, and why:**
- **The dock is the band's resting row.** Where yesterday's Gear and Menu sat (`dash/DashHeader.jsx` draws `menus3/Menus3Dock.jsx` instead under `?menus3`). Bro opens the Bag (Gear's root), World the Quests, People the Roster (today's Social hub). Every one is TODAY's screen in TODAY's band, never an old screen squeezed into the new panel (the card); the Menu's eight doors stay one press away (Gear's root, "Menu ›"), so nothing a player could reach yesterday is lost. The band rests on the dock for the load and its fold is never written (`dashMinBus.quiet`), so `?menus3=0` finds the band as it was.
- **Sideways, out of the thumbs' zones** (decided 2): the folded row runs under the world, the stick's disc over its left end and the Attack cluster over its right, so the three buttons sit compact in its middle, 96 px each, the corners empty. The owner's to change.
- **The shortcuts** (decided 3): the face opens Bro's page (Power), the weapon's bar that weapon's page, the minimap the world map, the quest line its quest, all as today; NEW: a round 44 px gear left of the minimap opens Settings (today's hub), placed off the minimap's own kept box (no measuring), and a tap on the chat lines opens the chat box (it used to fold the feed; the feed's header still folds it).
- **Upright, the fight controls step aside while a menu is open** (decided 1): `html.bt-menus3-hide-ctl`, the very block the sideways band uses (hidden and untouchable). Sideways and on a computer nothing new hides them (the sideways band still hides them over its own open page, as today).
- **The closes** (decided 1; numbers from the wireframe): the X, Esc (the X's `data-esc`), a tap on the world (moved under 10 px; on a computer that click is also the swing it always was), a swipe down on the open header (past 36 px; the drawer also right, past 42), and a hit taken outside a safe place (the hurt stamp `S.lastDamageTaken`, read once a frame; the Wheel's safe ground or a safe zone never closes). Rules: `src/game/menus3Close.js`.
- **The panel** (`menus3/Menus3Panel.jsx`, its shape `src/game/menus3Panel.js`): upright 48% of the screen held to 318..410 above the dock; sideways and on a computer a right drawer 40% wide; drawn as CSS, so nothing measures. No section opens it yet (the router's `built` is empty); the shell's test opens it by its probe. The wireframe's camera move (the world up 14% upright) and the controls' move left of the drawer wait for the step that first fills it.
- **Keys**: the owner's list is today's band's keys (I, P, L, J, K, U, M, C, B), so none is added; F (the Element Burst) and R (Whirlwind) are the game's already and are NOT taken (a flag for the owner).

## The new menus' Bag: the rail, the grid, Gear, the one Item page (v2.3.6861, card MN step 4)
Screens: menus3-bag, menus3-item

**What it is for:** what you carry, worn and kept, in the first of the owner's new menus to be really built (their document's "What You Carry" and decided 4: *"Gear is a TAB of the Bag (nine slots and the active pet), not pinned above the grid. Equip is two taps from the bag, and a slot's list of candidates has a direct Equip. Pets stay out of the All filter"*). The look is their wireframe's (v4.4), built from the game's kit.

**Decisions, and why:**
- **The panel goes live.** The Bro button opens the new panel on the Bag, always on All (the owner's rule 1: never where you left it); Bro pressed again closes it. Its header's Power and Skills open today's screens until their steps. Upright the panel is the shell's one height, the fight controls hidden, and the world's view moves up by the wireframe's 14% (the camera's aim, `menus3CamOffset`; never the world's scale): the bro stands above the panel. Sideways and on a computer the right drawer; the right-hand fight controls (the attack disc, the specials, jump, shield, sprint, ride, the trap) stand left of it, moved by the drawer's own 40% (`html.bt-menus3-drawer-open`, CSS `translate`: the canvas is not touched, nothing hidden); the stick and the Eat button were already left.
- **The tap that closes a menu is the menu's** (step 1's flaw, mended for every menus3 close, the band's too): a press that starts on the world while a menu is open is swallowed whole (its pointer, touch and mouse events and the click after it), so it neither walks, swings nor talks; a tap closes, a drag does nothing; the next press is the world's.
- **One Back, with its history** (`src/game/menus3Nav.js`, the piece every later section uses): a page opened inside the panel (an item, a slot, a pet) puts Back where the tabs were; Back goes one page; the page under it gets back its rail and its scroll (put back at the top of a frame). The X and every entry start again at the section's own page.
- **The rail** (the owner's seven): All with the bag's count and NO cap (the game has none: only the weapon bag has one), Gear (9), Weapons with the weapon bag's fill against the worker's cap, Armor, Consumables (food, potions), Materials (gems, ore, wood, crops, fish), Pets with the count owned. Quest items and Other are in All only (no rail of the six fits them). Pets are never in All. Rules: `src/game/menus3Bag.js`.
- **The grid** four across upright (as drawn), a computer's wide drawer as many whole 96 px tiles as fit; a tile the game's own picture (the BagTile today's Gear draws: the grade's edge, the count, the pin's anchor, the "?"), its name under it; identical spare armour one tile with its count, as Gear.
- **The one Item page** is today's item page's model (`itemPageInput`, `inspectItem`) drawn in the panel: the picture, name, grade, its facts; the game's own actions run by the one runner today's Gear runs (`runItemAction`: Equip, Unequip, Eat, Drink, Light fire, Reveal, Claim, Open a ticket, Inspect a fish, Pin; Drop opens the item card's own sheet); the lead one full width at the foot (Equip from the closed bag: Bro, the tile, Equip). What is done at a town counter (Sell at Diego's Shop, Smelt, Cook, Set gem, Harden, Bank) is shown greyed with where.
- **Gear, a tab**: the nine slots three across, each its worn piece or Empty, and the pet out with you; a slot's page: what is worn (its Item page a tap away) and every bag piece that fits, each with a direct Equip; the chest's page also opens today's loadout card (the shirt on or off).
- **Pets**: the Pets filter lists the pets you own; one opens TODAY's pet page in the panel (take out, put away, rename, release, sell where the store sells pets); the Pet House and the journal stay on today's Pets page, a door at the foot, until the Counter's Keep step.

**Flagged to the owner:** the wireframe's "All (11/40)" has a cap the game does not (the menu shows the count alone); the document's spare armour "32 per kind" is 48 in the worker (32 only under the big-bags kill switch); "Pin" is today's keep-it-first mark kept on the phone only (not saved), not a protection; the quick Eat button exists (over the stick); the item pictures load as today's Gear's do (the files, cached after the first look), not behind the loading screen.

Sources: src/game/menus3Nav.js, menus3Bag.js, menus3Panel.js (menus3CamOffset), cameraFollow.js (`oy`), src/ui/menus3/Menus3Bag.jsx, Menus3Panel.jsx, menus3Wire.js, menus3.css, src/styles/game.css (the drawer's controls), src/ui/mobile/dash/PetsPage.jsx (PetOne); server/test/menus3-bag.test.mjs; tools/qa/mp/mp-menus3bag.mjs.

## The new menus' Quests: the Today strip, the rail, a quest's page (v2.3.6863, card MN step 7)
Screens: menus3-quests, menus3-quest

**What it is for:** what to do next, in the owner's second built menu (their document's "What To Do": *"Your quests (tracked, active, ready to hand in)"*, *"Quests on offer (who & where)"*, *"Finished quests"*, *"Quest page (completed/current steps, rewards, Track, Show on map; future steps hidden)"*, *"Free daily spin & login streak: World · Quests (Today strip) + Gambling Den; top-bar indicator while a spin waits"*, *"Farm's 3 daily orders: World · Quests (Today strip to read; delivered at Feed & Seed)"*). The look is their wireframe's (v4.4): a rail over rows, a row's page with Back in the header.

**Decisions, and why:**
- **World opens the new panel on Quests, always** (rule 1); its tabs Quests | Codex | Feats and the X; Codex and Feats open today's screens until their steps. The quest line under the HUD (`?questline=1`, or the pinned card under `?hud=0`) opens THAT quest's page in the panel, its Back the Quests' own page.
- **The rail is Active | On offer | Done**, each with its count. NO Daily: the game's daily quests and the season are off (their caps are advertised only with their live flags on) and stay off; the wireframe's Daily rail is its sample. Active is the tracked quest first (its star), then the ones ready to hand in (Ready), each with its count where it counts; On offer says who and where; a row is today's row (the giver's face, the name), and a Map beside it (64 x 44: the wireframe's 39-wide "Go" mended) wherever the game can show the place. With nothing in hand and an offer waiting, the section opens on On offer (the old landing listed offers under its empty line). Rules: `src/game/menus3Quests.js` questRails, questRailStart (ordering by today's `questLanding`).
- **The Today strip holds only what exists today**: the free daily spin (the login wheel; lit with a dot while it waits; its words carry Spin, which opens the wheel exactly as the top bar's button does, the wheel's own request; the top bar's mark stays), the login streak with its freezes, the save, how it works, and the Gambling Den's spin in the Daily Rewards window's own words (asked fresh as that window asked, `rewards_get`), and the farm's three orders (read; "Delivered at Feed & Seed"). A place's words open under the strip, one at a time, so the rows keep the room. Under `?menus3` this carries everything the Daily Rewards window showed today (its Today tab: the season tab and the daily quests are off), so the window is not reached from Quests; should the daily quests or the season come on, the strip grows a "Daily rewards" door to the window, which draws them.
- **The farm's board is read, never asked for**: the phone learns it only from Feed & Seed (`farm_open`, which writes the free deed the first time), so before the board is seen, or once a new day is up, the strip says the orders are on the board at Feed & Seed.
- **A quest's page is today's drawing** (`questViews.jsx` QuestPageView, its rules `journeyToNow`, `questActions`): the steps behind you, NOW with its count, bar and place, "The adventure continues", and nothing still to come; Track (at once), Show on map (only where the game can), the Rewards door (a page over the quest: Back returns to it). Show on map opens today's map OVER the panel, which stays: closing the map is back on the quest.
- **Its links** come from the quest's own data, never from its words: the monsters whose remains it asks for (today's Codex page for each), the item (today's item page, "Drops from" the first of them), its place (the map). They open today's pages in today's band until the new Codex and Map (steps 8); the panel's history is kept as a ticket, and when their Back leaves the band the panel opens again on the quest (a Close keeps it shut): `menus3Wire.js` menus3Away.

**Flagged to the owner:** the farm's orders cannot be read before the player has visited Feed & Seed today (a request would make the free deed for a player who never farmed); a quest whose item nobody drops (the ore, the log) has no item link (its page would read "Drops from" no one); counted quests ("progress") have no monster or item in their data, so only their place links.

Sources: src/game/menus3Quests.js, menus3Router.js (MENUS3_BUILT), src/ui/menus3/Menus3Quests.jsx, Menus3Panel.jsx, menus3Wire.js (menus3Away, the quest line), menus3.css, src/ui/QuestCard.jsx (openQuestPage), src/data/say/menus3Quests.js; server/test/menus3-quests.test.mjs; tools/qa/mp/mp-menus3quests.mjs.

## The new menus' Power: the lanes, the draft, the stats (v2.3.6862, card MN step 5)
Screens: menus3-power

**What it is for:** spending your points and reading your numbers, in the owner's second rebuilt section (their document's "You" and decided 5: *"A DRAFT: tap a stat to stage a point, a minus to unstage, one 'Apply N points'. No Undo after Apply (the worker's Reset is the way back)... Which points are spent first (a weapon's or the shared) is whatever the worker does today: the menu shows it, never decides it."*). It replaces, under `?menus3`, the Combat hub, the three weapon pages, the Shared page and the Points window as the place points are placed.

**Decisions, and why:**
- **Every entry opens the same place** (rule 1): the face and the level, the Power tab and the weapon bar open the new panel on Power; the bar always on the weapon in hand's lane (decided 3); the others on the hand's lane unless it has nothing waiting and another lane has (the document's "or whichever lane has unspent points"), the first such in the lanes' order. The lane is the root page's `rail`, so a page opened from it gives it back.
- **Four lanes** (Sword, Bow, Staff, Body): a weapon's level, its XP as a thin bar and what is left to place; Body says Shared. The overall level at the top with its XP: the character's level is the best weapon's (`onelevel`), so its XP is that weapon's; there is no other overall XP in the game. A gold dot on Power's tab and on each lane with points waiting; Bro's own count is the Menu's mark it already wore.
- **The draft is the worker's arithmetic** (`src/game/menus3Power.js`): a weapon upgrade draws that weapon's point, else a spare one; a body upgrade a shared point, else a spare; a spare buys anything, so the draft is ordered and replayed every time, and a stage the worker would refuse (no points of its kinds, the stat at its bound) is not added and is said in one line. The kinds are told apart above the cards (Sword points 2 -> 1, Shared points, Spare points), and a card drawing a spare says so.
- **Cards two across** as the wireframe draws them: the card is the stage (its name, its points, "now -> after" while staged, its filled steps of its cap, or "no cap" where the game has none: `caps.nostatcap`), the minus beside it (44 wide, 8 px apart: the wireframe's 6 mended), disabled with nothing staged. Under the cards the last card touched: its number now and after the draft (today's preview, `statPreview.js`) and "What it does": today's window for it, read-only (the glossary, the rows, the replayed fight); points are placed only by the draft.
- **ONE Apply** sends the game's existing `prog3_allocate` through the action door, one point at a time: the next goes when the stat's points rose; no answer within today's wait (3 s, `STAY_WAIT_MS`: the worker's refusal is a quiet state resend) stops it and says so; no line to the server stops it before anything goes. What landed stays. Nothing is sent before Apply. The arrow beside it clears the draft.
- **Leaving drops the draft** (a tab, the X, a close): said in one quiet line over the bro, the points reset's own way of saying; no dialog. A queue under way finishes its point in flight and sends no more.
- **The stats line** is the footer's left (the wireframe's): the lane's headline number (DPS for a weapon, after the draft too; Max HP for the body), opening the lane's real numbers, today's "All stats" (`broWeaponStats` in `broPages.js`'s groups: a weapon's eight offence numbers; the body's Max HP, defence, armour, dodge, element resistance, move speed). DPS opens its breakdown, today's tree (`StatTree.jsx`, drawn in the panel, one layer a page, Back layer by layer); every other number its explainer, as today.
- **The free Reset** stays where the document puts it: a quiet button at the top, two taps (today's Points window's), shown only when the worker resets and a point is placed.

**Flagged to the owner:** the document's "Undo while the panel stays open" is the draft now (decided 5); the game has no overall XP of its own (the best weapon's is shown); the wireframe's four stats a lane are the game's six (and the body's seven); the stats line's "13 numbers" are split by lane (a weapon's eight, the body's six); the P key still opens today's Bro page (the keys wait for their own step).

Sources: src/game/menus3Power.js, menus3Router.js; src/ui/menus3/Menus3Power.jsx, Menus3Panel.jsx, menus3Wire.js, menus3.css; src/ui/mobile/sheet/StatTree.jsx (`at`); src/ui/hud/HudArt.jsx, CharacterHud.jsx; server/test/menus3-power.test.mjs; tools/qa/mp/mp-menus3power.mjs.

## The new menus' Roster and the one Player page (v2.3.6864, card MN step 10)
Screens: menus3-roster, menus3-player

**What it is for:** the people you play with, in the owner's third built menu (their document's "Other Players": *"Friends, requests, blocked, Add friend, invite link"*, *"Players near you"* with the top-left mini player list removed, *"Party (members, leader, invite, leave)"* pinned at the top with the HUD party chip opening it, *"Alliances: 1 line below Party bar"*, and the *"Unified Player Page"* from any player tap). Its layout is the wireframe v4.4's Roster and player page; its lists are the game's.

**Decisions, and why:**
- **People always opens Roster, on Nearby** (the owner's rule 1); its header's Chat and Ranks open today's chat box and Ranks until their step. The party chip opens Roster too, and so does every door that opened the old top-left list (the Saloon's "Invite a player", the Sheriff's "Duel a player", the Party page's button: `playersOpen`, one line in the router, `game.players`). Under `?menus3` that list never shows; Nearby carries all it did (the name, the Hemi Bro count, a tap to the player) and more (the level where the peer carries it, In your party, Friend, an Invite).
- **The party bar is pinned.** Alone it is ONE slim line, "No party · Play together", with Create (decided 8). The game has no request that makes a party: one starts when a player you invite says yes. So Create is today's road, not a new request: it lights Nearby, where every row has its Invite, and says so. In a party: its size from the worker, the members (the leader first, then you; a member opens their page), Invite while there is room, and Leave in two taps (as the chip's). The leader's Remove is on a member's page, also two taps; the chip's open list (health bars, its own kick and leave, opening by itself when someone needs you) is unchanged.
- **The Alliance line shows only what the chip and the Party page show today:** a party that is an alliance (`[TAG] name · Alliance · N active`) or wears its former clan's tag. Alliances are ON in today's worker, but a party under twenty active members has no line; how to grow one stays in the Saloon.
- **The segments are the game's lists with their counts:** Nearby (every other player the phone holds, as the old list: it never asked how far; your party first, then friends, then by name; a blocked player is under Blocked), Friends (today's friend rows: online, where, last seen; a Message beside a name the whisper lane can carry; Add friend with its how-to and the invite link; search past eight friends, as today), Requests (Accept and Decline, then the ones you sent, pending; a dot on the segment while one waits, never a jump to it), Blocked (each with Unblock, which today's Friends page lacked and the player card had).
- **One Player page, for any player** (`Menus3Roster.jsx` Menus3Player; `menus3OpenPlayer(id)` is the one call the Chat and Ranks steps make). It shows only what the phone already has (the full remote page stays off, SPEC "Server-gated features"): the name with its tag, the level, where (nearby, online in a land, last seen), In your party, Friend, the Hemi Bro count. Its actions are today's player card's, under the card's gates, each sending the card's own request: Message, Invite to party, Remove from party (the leader), Trade (the two-sided window; the panel goes, as its drawer joins the bag), Duel (no stake, as the card), Add or Remove friend, Player card (today's card, whose portrait opens the Inspect card: gear, points, kills), Mute, Block or Unblock (no Block where the land is lawless), Report with its four reasons. A player the phone does not hold has no card, trade, duel or invite (the card is only ever opened on one in the world).
- **"Message" is today's chat box aimed at the player** (decided 7): the whisper lane with their name; the panel shuts while it is up, and when the chat box shuts (its close, Esc, or a line sent) the panel comes back on their page. A whisper goes to a name with no spaces (the chat's rule), so a name with a space has no Message.
- **A player is a drawn disc** in their colour with their initial: no art loads because a menu opened (the old list's icon and the card's portrait are built from the look sheets).

**Flagged to the owner:**
- There is no "Create party" request; Create lights Nearby (see above).
- (v2.3.6870: the thread moved to People · Chat · Whispers.) A friend's private messages (the Friends page's thread, `friend_dm`) and the Guilds door live on today's Social page, reached from the Roster's last row until the Chat step (Whispers) and the Skills step (Guilds) carry them; Chat and Ranks keep their today's screens.
- Clans: the clan windows already turn into the party; a former clan's tag still rides on its party (the chip, the name plates, the card, the Alliance line here). Nothing of it is deleted.
- R (Roster in the owner's keys) is Whirlwind in the game: no key was added.

Sources: src/game/menus3Roster.js, menus3Router.js (MENUS3_BUILT, `hud.partyChip`, `game.players`), src/ui/menus3/Menus3Roster.jsx, Menus3Panel.jsx, menus3Wire.js (menus3AwayChat, menus3ChatShut), menus3.css, src/ui/panels/PartyHUD.jsx (the chip), src/ui/BroTown.jsx (playersOpen, the Saloon's and the Hall's doors, the chat's shutting), src/ui/mobile/sheet/friendsModel.js (addFriend), src/data/say/menus3Roster.js; server/test/menus3-roster.test.mjs; tools/qa/mp/mp-menus3roster.mjs.

## The new menus' Chat and Ranks (v2.3.6870, card MN step 11)
Screens: menus3-chat, menus3-ranks

**What it is for:** talking and comparing, in the owner's People menu (their document: *"Chat (All, Area, Party, Whisper): People · Chat (HUD chat lines or tapping own bro opens it; unified single chat box across mobile & desktop)"*, *"Friend messages: People · Chat · Whispers"*, *"Game news & unread count: People · Chat · News (badge on People dock button)"*, *"Ranks (combat, life skills, feats, records): People · Ranks (row opens that player's Player Page)"*).

**Decisions, and why:**
- **ONE chat box** for a phone and a computer: People · Chat in the panel. The play screen's chat lines, a tap on your own bro (today's ChatBubble) and the Chat tab open it. The play screen's lines STAY (decided 3). The old boxes are still in the code for `?menus3=0` (the cleanup step removes them); the computer's C and Enter keys wait for the keys commit (deskKeys.js is Bro · Skills' until it lands).
- **The channels are the game's real lanes** (`src/game/menus3Chat.js` chatTabs): All (the log whole, exactly what the play screen's lines show, the game's own lines included), Area, Party and Whispers only where the worker carries the lane (Party also only in a party: chatChannel.js's own rule), News always (the game's own lines: a line with no name, gold in the feed). A forged or vanished tab is All.
- **A line leaves through the game's one sender** (chat.js sendChatMessage, the one both of today's composers use): the tab sets the lane (chatChannel.js setMode, setTo) and the sender spells it, refuses a party line with no party or a whisper with no name, and sends the game's own request by act(). No chat rule, mute, block or moderation changed.
- **The log is the wireframe's**: lines 5 apart at 13.5 px, the name bold in the player's own colour (chatColor.js) with the lane's picture, then the words; the newest at the foot, drawn bottom up (CSS column-reverse) so nothing measures the page to keep it in view. A player's name is a door to their Player page (44 tall: the wireframe's inline name mended), keeping the line's name for a player the phone does not hold.
- **The composer is the wireframe's**: the Emotes smiley at the left (today's picker; the panel steps aside for it and comes back on Chat when it shuts, the Roster's old "Message" way), the box (44 tall), today's microphone where the browser has speech to text (never drawn where it has not), Send at the right. Drafts are kept per channel while the page is up.
- **Whispers** (the wireframe draws no "to" box; the game's whisper goes to ONE NAME, so the tab has it): the whisper lines, the name box, and the friends' threads (friend_dm, moved from the Social page: a rail of mutual friends, unread first with their counts; a thread is its own page with Back, today's archive, its send and its unread bookkeeping).
- **"Message" on a Player page** (decided 7) opens Chat on Whispers with the name set, over the Player page: Back returns to it.
- **News is read only**: opening it marks the game's lines seen (the mark the Notifications page and the play screen's feed share), and it carries the Notifications page's "Show on screen" for the play screen's feed.
- **The dots say why** (decided 7): People lights for a friend's message waiting, else for news unseen; the panel's Chat tab lights the same; inside Chat the tab with the reason (Whispers, News). The dock redraws only when the answer changes.
- **Ranks is the wireframe's rail over the standings**, from today's boards: the kinds the game keeps (Combat, Life Skills, Feats: today's groups; the records, the spin, the flip and the fish, are Feats' own there), the kind's boards as a second rail (the stat boards at the end of Combat's, one tap instead of today's "Points placed" door), today's standings under them (dash/RankStandings.jsx: your row marked and pinned when out of view, the record boards' choices), the pick and scroll kept for Back. A row opens the one Player page (its name and the row's own card, today's Character page, for a player the phone does not hold); your own row opens Bro · Power, as today's opened Bro's page.

**Flagged to the owner:**
- The wireframe's rails say Combat, Skills, Feats, Records; the game's Ranks keeps three kinds (Combat, Life Skills, Feats) with the records inside Feats: the game's are shown.
- The wireframe's Whispers has no name box; the game needs one (a whisper goes to one name).
- A chat name 44 tall makes a player's line taller than the wireframe's.
- C (Chat), U (Ranks), B (Emotes): the keys wait for Bro · Skills' deskKeys.js; until then C opens today's chat box and B today's picker, as yesterday.

Sources: src/game/menus3Chat.js, menus3Router.js (MENUS3_BUILT), menus3Roster.js (`rowCard`); src/ui/menus3/Menus3Chat.jsx, Menus3Ranks.jsx, menus3People.js, Menus3Panel.jsx, Menus3Dock.jsx, Menus3Roster.jsx (Message, menus3PlayerHint), menus3Wire.js, menus3.css; src/ui/mobile/dash/RankStandings.jsx (`nav`), LeaderboardPanel.jsx (ui2RankParts); src/ui/panels/EmotePanel.jsx; src/ui/BroTown.jsx (openSelfChat); src/data/say/menus3Chat.js; server/test/menus3-chat.test.mjs; tools/qa/mp/mp-menus3chat.mjs.
